17351:   function stationsForOrder(reference, onlyActiveAssociations = false){
17352:     const found = resolveOrder(reference);
17353:     const item = found?.item || unwrap(reference);
17354:     const stations = [];
17355:     const principal = clean(item?.estacion || found?.row?.estacion);
17356:     if (principal) stations.push({ nombre: principal, rol: 'Principal', ramal: clean(item?.ramal), sector: clean(item?.sector) });
17357:     associationsForOrder(reference)
17358:       .filter((association) => !onlyActiveAssociations || associationIsActive(association))
17359:       .forEach((association) => {
17360:         const name = clean(association?.estacion);
17361:         if (!name) return;
17362:         const key = normalizeStation(name);
17363:         const existing = stations.find((station) => normalizeStation(station.nombre) === key);
17364:         const role = association?.es_principal ? 'Principal' : 'Asociada';
17365:         if (existing) {
17366:           if (role === 'Principal') existing.rol = 'Principal';
17367:           return;
17368:         }
17369:         stations.push({
17370:           nombre: name,
17371:           rol: role,
17372:           ramal: clean(association?.ramal),
17373:           sector: clean(association?.sector),
17374:           association
17375:         });
17376:       });
17377:     return stations;
17378:   }

17466:   function patchOrdersRender(){
17467:     const previousRender = window.renderOrdenes;
17468:     if (typeof previousRender === 'function') {
17469:       window.renderOrdenes = function(...args){

17473:         ordersRenderLock = true;

17475:           if (select && selected) select.value = '';

17477:           if (select) select.value = selected;
17478:           setTimeout(applyOrdersTypeFilter, 25);
17479:           return result;
17480:         } finally {
17481:           ordersRenderLock = false;
17482:         }
17483:       };
17484:     }
17485: 
17486:     const oldFilter = window.filtrarOrdenes;
17487:     if (typeof oldFilter === 'function') {
17488:       window.filtrarOrdenes = function(rows){
17489:         const select = $('ordenesFiltroTipo');
17490:         const selected = clean(select?.value);
17491:         if (select && selected) select.value = '';
17492:         let result;
17493:         try { result = oldFilter(rows); }
17494:         finally { if (select) select.value = selected; }
17495:         if (!selected) return result;
17496:         const wanted = normalizarTipoOrden(selected);
17497:         return (Array.isArray(result) ? result : []).filter((row) => clasificarOrdenCOI(row) === wanted);
17498:       };
17499:     }

23809:     const list=rows.filter(p=>normalizarOC(p.ocNro||p.nro_oc)===oc);
23810:     const masters=list.filter(p=>fold(p.tipoBloqueFinanciero)==='POSICION_OC');
23811:     const certs=list.filter(p=>fold(p.tipoBloqueFinanciero).includes('CERT'));
23812:     let total=0,certified=0,free=0,freeCount=0;
23813:     for(const master of masters){
23814:       const pos=normalizarPOS(master.posicion);

23819:     }

7200: function formatearMonedaARS(valor){

7199: /* V59 - Ficha OC/UM V56 */
7200: function formatearMonedaARS(valor){
7201:   if(valor===undefined||valor===null) return '—';
7202:   let s=String(valor).trim();
7203:   if(!s||s==='—') return '—';
7204:   s=s.replace(/[^\d,.\-]/g,'');
7205:   if(!s||s==='-'||s===','||s==='.') return '—';
7206:   const lastComma=s.lastIndexOf(',');
7207:   const lastDot=s.lastIndexOf('.');
7208:   let normalized=s;
7209:   if(lastComma>lastDot){
7210:     normalized=s.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.');
7211:   }else if(lastDot>lastComma){
7212:     normalized=s.replace(/,/g,'');
7213:   }
7214:   const n=Number(normalized);
7215:   if(!Number.isFinite(n)) return '—';
7216:   return new Intl.NumberFormat('es-AR',{style:'currency',currency:'ARS',minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}).format(n).replace(/\u00a0/g,' ');
7217: }

7351:     reader.onload=()=>{
7352:       const raw=String(reader.result||'');
7353:       const img=new Image();
7354:       img.onload=()=>{
7355:         try{
7356:           const scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.width,img.height));
7357:           if(scale>=1){resolve(raw);return;}
7358:           const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
7359:           canvas.width=Math.round(img.width*scale);canvas.height=Math.round(img.height*scale);
7360:           const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
7361:           resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',quality));
7362:         }catch(e){resolve(raw);}
7363:       };
7364:       img.onerror=()=>resolve(raw);
7365:       img.src=raw;
7366:     };
7367:     reader.onerror=()=>resolve('');
7368:     reader.readAsDataURL(file);
7369:   });

7348: function v59ImagenADataUrl(file,max=1280,quality=.82){
7349:   return new Promise(resolve=>{
7350:     const reader=new FileReader();
7351:     reader.onload=()=>{
7352:       const raw=String(reader.result||'');
7353:       const img=new Image();
7354:       img.onload=()=>{
7355:         try{
7356:           const scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.width,img.height));
7357:           if(scale>=1){resolve(raw);return;}
7358:           const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
7359:           canvas.width=Math.round(img.width*scale);canvas.height=Math.round(img.height*scale);
7360:           const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
7361:           resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',quality));
7362:         }catch(e){resolve(raw);}
7363:       };
7364:       img.onerror=()=>resolve(raw);
7365:       img.src=raw;
7366:     };
7367:     reader.onerror=()=>resolve('');
7368:     reader.readAsDataURL(file);
7369:   });
7370: }

7739:   if(!valor)return null;
7740:   if(valor instanceof Date)return isNaN(valor)?null:new Date(valor.getFullYear(),valor.getMonth(),valor.getDate());
7741:   const s=String(valor).trim();
7742:   if(!s||s==='—'||normalizarTexto(s)==='SIN DATOS')return null;
7743:   let m=s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})/);
7744:   if(m){const d=new Date(Number(m[1]),Number(m[2])-1,Number(m[3]));return isNaN(d)?null:d;}

7748: }

5654: function estadoSemaforoOC(item,calc){
5655:   if(esCerradaPyC(item) || item.estadoRegistro==='Archivado' || item.estadoRegistro==='Cerrado') return {clase:'closed',texto:'Cerrado / Archivado'};

5658:   return {clase:'ok',texto:'En plazo'};
5659: }
